diary on;

try
    %% 加载现有模型
    S1 = load(fullfile(modelDir, 'eciML1515_models_part1.mat'), ...
        'models_part1');
    S2 = load(fullfile(modelDir, 'eciML1515_models_part2.mat'), ...
        'models_part2');

    part1Names = fieldnames(S1.models_part1);
    part2Names = fieldnames(S2.models_part2);

    eciML1515_models = S1.models_part1;
    for i = 1:numel(part2Names)
        name = part2Names{i};
        eciML1515_models.(name) = S2.models_part2.(name);
    end

    %% 只使用 UnconstrainedMaxGrowth.tsv 的 Substrate 列
    T = readtable( ...
        fullfile(calDir, 'data', 'UnconstrainedMaxGrowth.tsv'), ...
        'FileType', 'text', 'ReadRowNames', true);

    exList = unique(string(T.Substrate), 'stable');
    exList = exList(~ismissing(exList) & strlength(exList) > 0 ...
                    & ~strcmpi(exList, "NaN"));
    exList = cellstr(exList);

    fprintf('交换反应数量：%d\n', numel(exList));

    %% 为基础模型补齐缺失的水闸分支
    % 已有两个分支的反应不重复调用 applySluiceStructure。
    names = fieldnames(eciML1515_models);
    baseNames = names(~endsWith(names, '_GAUKS'));

    for i = 1:numel(baseNames)
        name = baseNames{i};
        m = eciML1515_models.(name);

        if ~any(strcmp(m.mets, 'prot_pool'))
            error('%s 缺少 prot_pool，无法应用水闸结构。', name);
        end

        missing = {};

        for j = 1:numel(exList)
            ex = exList{j};

            if ~any(strcmp(m.rxns, ex))
                error('%s 缺少原始交换反应 %s。', name, ex);
            end

            hasBasal = any(strcmp(m.rxns, [ex '_basal']));
            hasExtended = any(strcmp(m.rxns, [ex '_extended']));

            if xor(hasBasal, hasExtended)
                error('%s 的 %s 只有一个水闸分支，请检查模型。', ...
                    name, ex);
            end

            if ~hasBasal
                missing{end+1} = ex; %#ok<AGROW>
            end
        end

        if ~isempty(missing)
            fprintf('\n%s：补齐 %d 个交换反应的水闸结构\n', ...
                name, numel(missing));
            [m, ~] = applySluiceStructure(m, missing);
            eciML1515_models.(name) = m;
        else
            fprintf('%s：水闸结构已齐全\n', name);
        end
    end

    %% 从对应基础模型重新运行 GAUKS
    GEM = loadModel('iML1515.xml', 'Tradition', modelDir);
    gauksNames = names(endsWith(names, '_GAUKS'));
    gauksAudit = struct();

    for i = 1:numel(gauksNames)
        gauksName = gauksNames{i};
        baseName = erase(gauksName, '_GAUKS');

        if ~isfield(eciML1515_models, baseName)
            error('%s 找不到对应的基础模型 %s。', ...
                gauksName, baseName);
        end

        fprintf('\n========== %d/%d：%s ==========\n', ...
            i, numel(gauksNames), gauksName);
        fprintf('输入基础模型：%s\n', baseName);

        [mGauks, ~, summaryTbl] = GAUKS( ...
            eciML1515_models.(baseName), GEM, ...
            'BIOMASS_Ec_iML1515_core_75p37M');

        % 检查所有指定交换反应的水闸分支
        for j = 1:numel(exList)
            ex = exList{j};
            if ~any(strcmp(mGauks.rxns, [ex '_basal'])) || ...
               ~any(strcmp(mGauks.rxns, [ex '_extended']))
                error('%s 仍缺少 %s 的水闸分支。', gauksName, ex);
            end
        end

        eciML1515_models.(gauksName) = mGauks;

        %% 记录本模型的校准状态
        xiOK = ismember(string(summaryTbl.XiStatus), ...
            ["Converged", "AlreadyMatched"]);
        qmaxOK = ismember(string(summaryTbl.QmaxStatus), ...
            ["Solved", "BoundAt1000"]);
        numericOK = isfinite(summaryTbl.Umin) & ...
                    isfinite(summaryTbl.Error_Percent);

        problemRows = summaryTbl( ...
            ~(xiOK & qmaxOK & numericOK), ...
            {'Substrate', 'Umin', 'QmaxPre', 'QmaxStatus', ...
             'ProteomicCost', 'XiStatus', 'Error_Percent'});

        gauksAudit.(gauksName).summaryTbl = summaryTbl;
        gauksAudit.(gauksName).problemRows = problemRows;
        gauksAudit.(gauksName).nTotal = height(summaryTbl);
        gauksAudit.(gauksName).nPassed = ...
            nnz(xiOK & qmaxOK & numericOK);

        fprintf('\n%s：通过初步检查 %d/%d 条\n', ...
            gauksName, gauksAudit.(gauksName).nPassed, ...
            gauksAudit.(gauksName).nTotal);

        fprintf('XiStatus 统计：\n');
        disp(groupcounts(summaryTbl, 'XiStatus'));

        fprintf('QmaxStatus 统计：\n');
        disp(groupcounts(summaryTbl, 'QmaxStatus'));

        if ~isempty(problemRows)
            fprintf('需要检查的条件：\n');
            disp(problemRows);
        end
    end

    %% 恢复原 part1、part2 字段分组
    models_part1 = struct();
    for i = 1:numel(part1Names)
        name = part1Names{i};
        models_part1.(name) = eciML1515_models.(name);
    end

    models_part2 = struct();
    for i = 1:numel(part2Names)
        name = part2Names{i};
        models_part2.(name) = eciML1515_models.(name);
    end

    %% 普通 save；另存新文件，不覆盖原文件
    save(fullfile(modelDir, 'eciML1515_models_part1_rebuilt.mat'), ...
        'models_part1');
    save(fullfile(modelDir, 'eciML1515_models_part2_rebuilt.mat'), ...
        'models_part2');
    save(fullfile(calDir, 'GAUKS_rebuild_audit.mat'), ...
        'gauksAudit');

    fprintf('\n运行结束。\n日志：%s\n', logFile);
    fprintf('状态表：%s\n', ...
        fullfile(calDir, 'GAUKS_rebuild_audit.mat'));

catch ME
    fprintf(2, '\n运行中断：%s\n', ME.message);
    diary off;
    rethrow(ME);
end
